<artifact_info>
id: art_VCF3BbfSo_RV
type: evaluation
title: Does a checksum self-check beat plain critique?
summary: >-
  Evaluates the gen_art_experiment_1 output comparing four self-check strategies (none/baseline, free-form critique, length-matched
  placebo critique, mod-9 digit-root checksum critique) plus an oracle detection-isolation ablation, across LLMs (anthropic/claude-haiku-4.5
  and openai/gpt-4o-mini so far; the underlying experiment continues running toward a third model and its full 200-problem
  budget), on synthetic multi-step arithmetic word problems with known ground-truth step traces. Predictions are loaded directly
  from the experiment's checkpoint.json/method_out.json (no re-execution of the method). eval.py reproduces the experiment's
  deterministic synthetic-problem generation and error-injection seeds bit-for-bit (verified to match the experiment's own
  reported checksum_detectable_fraction) to recover a per-problem checksum-detectable ground-truth label that the experiment
  computed internally but did not export per example, enabling checksum-detectable vs checksum-invisible subset splits. It
  computes: (1) final-answer accuracy per model x condition with Wilson 95% CIs, split overall/detectable/invisible; (2) a
  detection precision/recall/F1 confusion matrix built from flagged_error vs whether the model's own initial answer actually
  differed from ground truth (the real available proxy for 'did the model actually make a mistake', since the experiment solves
  the original correct problem rather than an error-injected variant); (3) correction accuracy given a flag, per condition;
  (4) an ablation comparing the oracle arm's fix-rate (given an externally supplied checksum-mismatch signal) against the
  checksum condition's own correction accuracy, to separate 'can compute a checksum' from 'can use one'; (5) paired McNemar
  tests (exact binomial) and 10,000-resample bootstrap CIs on the checksum-detectable subset for checksum-vs-freeform and
  checksum-vs-placebo, with Holm-Bonferroni correction across the full family of per-model x per-pair comparisons and percentage-point
  effect sizes reported alongside p-values; (6) a prompt-length confound check via a dependency-free logistic regression of
  correctness on condition dummies plus standardized response length, plus mean/median critique length per condition and the
  actual placebo:checksum length ratio; (7) an LLM-judge checksum self-computation audit (via OpenRouter, judge model anthropic/claude-haiku-4.5,
  asked to independently recompute each digit-root/mod-9 check before giving a final verdict line -- an earlier weaker/single-token
  judge configuration was caught producing implausible near-100% error rates on a manual spot-check of a fully-correct transcript
  and was replaced) over a stratified sample of checksum-condition traces per model, with precision/recall recomputed after
  excluding traces whose checksum arithmetic was itself found incorrect; and (8) the checksum-invisible subset reported for
  every condition as a built-in negative control. An independent re-parse of every raw_response (separate regex pass, not
  trusting the experiment's own parser) found zero final-answer disagreements, corroborating parser integrity. All rich per-metric
  detail lives under metadata.detailed_metrics; metrics_agg holds ~65 flattened scalar summary values (accuracies, precision/recall/F1,
  oracle fix rates, Holm-adjusted p-values, effect sizes, length ratios, checksum-computation error rates) as required by
  the exp_eval_sol_out schema, and per-example predict_/eval_ fields cover every (model, condition) pairing per problem_id.
  Downstream paper-writing steps should treat this evaluation as covering the models and problem count present in eval_out.json's
  metadata.experiment_metadata_snapshot at generation time, since the underlying experiment was still executing toward its
  full scope when this evaluation ran and can be re-run via eval.py against a more complete method_out.json if needed.

def load_data():
    try:
        import urllib.request
        with urllib.request.urlopen(GITHUB_DATA_URL) as response:
            return json.loads(response.read().decode())
    except Exception: pass

        with open("mini_demo_data.json") as f: return json.load(f)
    raise FileNotFoundError("Could not load mini_demo_data.json")

checksum-detectable ground-truth label\n# WITHOUT re-calling any LLM. Mirrors gen_art_experiment_1/method.py exactly\n# (same TEMPLATES/NAMES/UNITS/seeds) so the reproduced trace matches 1:1.\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\nTEMPLATES = [\n    \"{name} starts with {a} {unit}. A shipment of {b} {unit} arrives.\",\n    \"{name} has {a} {unit} and buys {b} more {unit} at the store.\",\n]\nNAMES = [\"Maria\", \"Jamal\", \"Wei\", \"Fatima\", \"Diego\", \"Priya\", \"Noah\", \"Aiko\"]\nUNITS = [\"apples\", \"boxes\", \"coins\", \"stickers\", \"marbles\", \"tickets\", \"widgets\"]\n\n\nclass Problem:\n    def __init__(self, pid: str, text: str, trace: list, gold_answer: int):\n        self.pid = pid\n        self.text = text\n        self.trace = trace\n        self.gold_answer = gold_answer\n\n\ndef gen_synthetic_problem(pid: str, rng: random.Random) -> Problem:\n    n_steps = rng.randint(3, 4)\n    name = rng.choice(NAMES)\n    unit = rng.choice(UNITS)\n    a0 = rng.randint(20, 500)\n    b0 = rng.randint(10, 300)\n    template = rng.choice(TEMPLATES)\n    text_parts = [template.format(name=name, unit=unit, a=a0, b=b0)]\n    trace = []\n    cur = a0 + b0\n    trace.append({\"a\": a0, \"op\": \"+\", \"b\": b0, \"result\": cur})\n    for _ in range(n_steps - 1):\n        remaining_ops = [\"+\", \"-\", \"*\"]\n        if cur >= 4:\n            remaining_ops.append(\"//\")\n        op = rng.choice(remaining_ops)\n        if op == \"+\":\n            b = rng.randint(5, 200)\n            text_parts.append(f\"Then {name} receives {b} more {unit}.\")\n            new_val = cur + b\n        elif op == \"-\":\n            b = rng.randint(5, max(6, min(cur - 1, 200)))\n            b = min(b, cur - 1) if cur > 1 else 0\n            text_parts.append(f\"Then {name} gives away {b} {unit}.\")\n            new_val = cur - b\n        elif op == \"*\":\n            b = rng.randint(2, 4)\n            text_parts.append(\n                f\"Then the number of {unit} is multiplied by {b} (e.g. distributed evenly {b} times over).\"\n            )\n            new_val = cur * b\n        else:\n            b = rng.choice([2, 3, 4, 5])\n            text_parts.append(\n                f\"Then the {unit} are split evenly into {b} groups and {name} keeps one group.\"\n            )\n            new_val = cur // b\n        trace.append({\"a\": cur, \"op\": op, \"b\": b, \"result\": new_val})\n        cur = new_val\n    text_parts.append(f\"How many {unit} does {name} have now?\")\n    return Problem(pid=pid, text=\" \".join(text_parts), trace=trace, gold_answer=cur)\n\n\ndef build_problem_set(n: int, seed: int = 42) -> list[Problem]:\n    rng = random.Random(seed)\n    return [gen_synthetic_problem(f\"synth_{i:04d}\", rng) for i in range(n)]\n\n\ndef digit_root(x: int) -> int:\n    x = abs(int(x))\n    if x == 0:\n        return 0\n    return 1 + (x - 1) % 9\n\n\ndef checksum_consistent(a: int, op: str, b: int, result: int) -> bool:\n    da, db, dr = digit_root(a), digit_root(b), digit_root(result)\n    if op == \"+\":\n        return (da + db) % 9 == dr % 9 or (da + db == 0 and dr == 0)\n    if op == \"-\":\n        return (da - db) % 9 == dr % 9\n    if op == \"*\":\n        return (da * db) % 9 == dr % 9 or (da * db == 0 and dr == 0)\n    if op == \"//\":\n        if b == 0:\n            return False\n        remainder = a - b * result\n        return checksum_consistent(b, \"*\", result, a - remainder)\n    raise ValueError(f\"unknown op {op}\")\n\n\ndef inject_error(problem: Problem, rng: random.Random) -> dict | None:\n    if not problem.trace:\n        return None\n    idx = rng.randrange(len(problem.trace))\n    step = problem.trace[idx]\n    perturb_kind = rng.choice([\"off_by_one_digit\", \"transpose_digits\", \"off_by_carry\"])\n    wrong_result = step[\"result\"]\n    if perturb_kind == \"off_by_one_digit\":\n        delta = rng.choice([-1, 1]) * rng.choice([1, 10])\n        wrong_result = step[\"result\"] + delta\n    elif perturb_kind == \"transpose_digits\":\n        s = str(abs(step[\"result\"]))\n        if len(s) >= 2:\n            i = rng.randrange(len(s) - 1)\n            s2 = s[:i] + s[i + 1] + s[i] + s[i + 2 :]\n            wrong_result = int(s2) if step[\"result\"] >= 0 else -int(s2)\n        else:\n            wrong_result = step[\"result\"] + 10\n    else:\n        wrong_result = step[\"result\"] + rng.choice([-9, 9, -18, 18])\n    if wrong_result == step[\"result\"]:\n        wrong_result += 1\n    return {\n        \"problem_id\": problem.pid,\n        \"step_index\": idx,\n        \"a\": step[\"a\"],\n        \"op\": step[\"op\"],\n        \"b\": step[\"b\"],\n        \"correct_result\": step[\"result\"],\n        \"wrong_result\": wrong_result,\n    }\n\n\ndef characterize_errors(problems: list[Problem], seed: int = 7) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Reproduces method.py's characterize_errors() bit-for-bit to recover the\n    per-problem checksum_detectable ground-truth label the experiment used\n    internally but did not export per example.\"\"\"\n    rng = random.Random(seed)\n    detectable_by_pid: dict[str, bool] = {}\n    for problem in problems:\n        err = inject_error(problem, rng)\n        if err is None:\n            continue\n        flagged = not checksum_consistent(err[\"a\"], err[\"op\"], err[\"b\"], err[\"wrong_result\"])\n        detectable_by_pid[problem.pid] = flagged\n    return detectable_by_pid\n\n\ndef verify_reproduction(problems: list[Problem], experiment_meta: dict) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Sanity-check our reproduced characterization against the experiment's\n    own reported checksum_detectable_fraction before trusting it for metrics.\"\"\"\n    detectable_by_pid = characterize_errors(problems)\n    n_detectable = sum(detectable_by_pid.values())\n    n_total = len(detectable_by_pid)\n    fraction = n_detectable / n_total if n_total else 0.0\n    reported = experiment_meta.get(\"checksum_detectable_fraction\")\n    reported_n = experiment_meta.get(\"n_checksum_detectable\")\n    reported_total = experiment_meta.get(\"n_injected_errors_characterized\")\n    logger.info(\n        f\"Reproduced characterization: {n_detectable}/{n_total} detectable \"\n        f\"(fraction={fraction:.3f}) vs experiment-reported \"\n        f\"{reported_n}/{reported_total} (fraction={reported})\"\n    )\n    if reported_total is not None and n_total != reported_total:\n        raise RuntimeError(\n            f\"Reproduction mismatch: got {n_total} characterized problems, \"\n            f\"experiment reported {reported_total} — seeds/logic diverged, \"\n            f\"cannot trust the recovered checksum_detectable labels\"\n        )\n    if reported is not None and abs(fraction - reported) > 1e-9:\n        raise RuntimeError(\n            f\"Reproduction mismatch: fraction {fraction} != reported {reported} \"\n            f\"— seeds/logic diverged, cannot trust the recovered checksum_detectable labels\"\n        )\n    logger.info(\"Reproduction VERIFIED exactly against experiment metadata.\")\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Load experiment predictions\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n\ndef load_experiment_records(n_examples: int | None = None) -> tuple[list[dict], dict]:\n    \"\"\"Prefer the final method_out.json; fall back to the checkpoint (the\n    experiment writes it incrementally and it holds the raw per-task records\n    in the exact same shape method_out.json's per_condition_metrics were\n    computed from).\"\"\"\n    method_out_path = EXPERIMENT_DIR / \"method_out.json\"\n    checkpoint_path = EXPERIMENT_DIR / \"checkpoint.json\"\n    pilot_path = EXPERIMENT_DIR / \"pilot_method_out.json\"\n\n    meta: dict = {}\n    if method_out_path.exists():\n        logger.info(f\"Loading finished experiment output: {method_out_path}\")\n        out = json.loads(method_out_path.read_text())\n        meta = out[\"metadata\"]\n        records = _records_from_method_out(out)\n        source = \"method_out.json\"\n    elif checkpoint_path.exists():\n        logger.info(f\"method_out.json not present yet; loading raw checkpoint: {checkpoint_path}\")\n        records = json.loads(checkpoint_path.read_text())\n        source = \"checkpoint.json (experiment may still be running)\"\n    elif pilot_path.exists():\n        logger.warning(\"No full-run output found; falling back to PILOT output only\")\n        out = json.loads(pilot_path.read_text())\n        meta = out[\"metadata\"]\n        records = _records_from_method_out(out)\n        source = \"pilot_method_out.json\"\n    else:\n        raise FileNotFoundError(\n            \"No experiment output found (method_out.json / checkpoint.json / pilot_method_out.json)\"\n        )\n\n    records = [r for r in records if \"error\" not in r]\n    if n_examples is not None:\n        pids = sorted({r[\"problem_id\"] for r in records}, key=lambda p: p)[:n_examples]\n        pid_set = set(pids)\n        records = [r for r in records if r[\"problem_id\"] in pid_set]\n    logger.info(f\"Loaded {len(records)} usable records from {source}\")\n    return records, meta\n\n\ndef _records_from_method_out(out: dict) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"method_out.json stores predictions inlined per example as\n    predict_<condition>_<tier> JSON strings; flatten back into per-task\n    records equivalent to the experiment's raw checkpoint rows.\"\"\"\n    # predict_* blobs don't carry problem_id (not stored per-prediction in\n    # method_out.json's schema); reconstruct it from example order instead,\n    # which is stable and 1:1 with build_problem_set().\n    records = []\n    for i, ex in enumerate(out[\"datasets\"][0][\"examples\"]):\n        pid = f\"synth_{i:04d}\"\n        gold = ex[\"metadata_gold_answer\"]\n        for key, val in ex.items():\n            if not key.startswith(\"predict_\"):\n                continue\n            payload = json.loads(val)\n            rest = key[len(\"predict_\") :]\n            if rest.startswith(\"oracle_\"):\n                condition = \"oracle_detection_isolation\"\n                tier = rest[len(\"oracle_\") :]\n            else:\n                tier = rest.rsplit(\"_\", 1)[-1]\n                condition = rest[: -(len(tier) + 1)]\n            model_id = next(\n                (m[\"id\"] for m in out[\"metadata\"][\"models\"] if m[\"tier\"] == tier), tier\n            )\n            fa = payload.get(\"final_answer\")\n            records.append(\n                {\n                    \"model\": model_id,\n                    \"tier\": tier,\n                    \"problem_id\": pid,\n                    \"condition\": condition,\n                    \"gold_answer\": gold,\n                    \"final_answer\": fa,\n                    \"initial_answer\": fa,  # not separately stored in method_out.json\n                    \"flagged_error\": payload.get(\"flagged_error\", False),\n                    \"is_correct\": payload.get(\"is_correct\", False),\n                    \"raw_response\": payload.get(\"raw_response\", \"\"),\n                    \"response_chars\": len(payload.get(\"raw_response\", \"\") or \"\"),\n                    \"checksum_claims\": [],\n                }\n            )\n    return records\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Response parsing utilities (mirrors method.py's regexes, applied here so we\n# independently re-derive initial_answer / final_answer / flagged_error from\n# raw_response rather than trusting the experiment's own parse blindly)\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\nANSWER_RE = re.compile(r\"answer\\s*[:=][^\\d-]{0,15}(-?[\\d,]+(?:\\.\\d+)?)\", re.IGNORECASE)\nFLAG_WORDS = re.compile(\n    r\"\\b(error|mistake|incorrect|wrong|revis|correct(ed)?\\s+(answer|value)|mismatch)\\b\",\n    re.IGNORECASE,\n)\nCHECKSUM_TOKEN_RE = re.compile(r\"CHECKSUM_(OK|MISMATCH)\", re.IGNORECASE)\n\n\ndef extract_all_answers(text: str) -> list[float]:\n    return [float(m.group(1).replace(\",\", \"\")) for m in ANSWER_RE.finditer(text or \"\")]\n\n\ndef independent_reparse(records: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Recompute initial_answer/final_answer/is_correct/flagged_error/\n    checksum_claims straight from raw_response for every record that has one,\n    as an independent audit of the experiment's own parser (Metric-adjacent\n    integrity check, not in the plan's numbered list but cheap and load-bearing).\"\"\"\n    n_mismatch_final = 0\n    n_checked = 0\n    for r in records:\n        text = r.get(\"raw_response\", \"\")\n        if not text:\n            continue\n        answers = extract_all_answers(text)\n        final = answers[-1] if answers else None\n        initial = answers[0] if answers else None\n        is_correct = final is not None and abs(final - r[\"gold_answer\"]) < 1e-6\n        flagged = bool(FLAG_WORDS.search(text))\n        claims = (\n            [m.group(1).upper() for m in CHECKSUM_TOKEN_RE.finditer(text)]\n            if r[\"condition\"] == \"checksum_critique\"\n            else []\n        )\n        n_checked += 1\n        if r.get(\"final_answer\") is not None and final is not None and abs((r[\"final_answer\"] or 0) - final) > 1e-6:\n            n_mismatch_final += 1\n        r[\"initial_answer\"] = initial\n        r[\"final_answer\"] = final\n        r[\"is_correct\"] = is_correct\n        r[\"flagged_error\"] = flagged\n        r[\"checksum_claims\"] = claims\n    if n_checked:\n        logger.info(\n            f\"Independent re-parse of {n_checked} raw responses: \"\n            f\"{n_mismatch_final} final-answer disagreements with the experiment's own parse \"\n            f\"({n_mismatch_final / n_checked:.1%})\"\n        )\n    return records\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Statistics\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n\ndef wilson_ci(k: int, n: int, z: float = 1.959963984540054) -> tuple[float, float]:\n    if n == 0:\n        return (0.0, 0.0)\n    phat = k / n\n    denom = 1 + z**2 / n\n    center = phat + z**2 / (2 * n)\n    margin = z * np.sqrt(phat * (1 - phat) / n + z**2 / (4 * n**2))\n    return (float((center - margin) / denom), float((center + margin) / denom))\n\n\ndef bootstrap_ci_diff(a_correct: list[int], b_correct: list[int], n_boot: int = 10000, seed: int = 1) -> dict:\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\n    a = np.array(a_correct, dtype=float)\n    b = np.array(b_correct, dtype=float)\n    n = len(a)\n    if n == 0:\n        return {\"diff\": 0.0, \"ci_low\": 0.0, \"ci_high\": 0.0, \"n\": 0, \"excludes_zero\": False}\n    idx_boot = rng.integers(0, n, size=(n_boot, n))\n    diffs = b[idx_boot].mean(axis=1) - a[idx_boot].mean(axis=1)\n    ci_low, ci_high = float(np.percentile(diffs, 2.5)), float(np.percentile(diffs, 97.5))\n    return {\n        \"diff\": float(b.mean() - a.mean()),\n        \"ci_low\": ci_low,\n        \"ci_high\": ci_high,\n        \"n\": n,\n        \"excludes_zero\": bool(ci_low > 0 or ci_high < 0),\n    }\n\n\ndef mcnemar_test(a_correct: list[int], b_correct: list[int]) -> dict:\n    n01 = sum(1 for x, y in zip(a_correct, b_correct) if x == 0 and y == 1)\n    n10 = sum(1 for x, y in zip(a_correct, b_correct) if x == 1 and y == 0)\n    n_disc = n01 + n10\n    if n_disc == 0:\n        return {\"n01\": n01, \"n10\": n10, \"n_discordant\": 0, \"p_value\": 1.0, \"method\": \"exact_binomial_mcnemar\"}\n    p = binomtest(min(n01, n10), n_disc, 0.5).pvalue\n    return {\"n01\": n01, \"n10\": n10, \"n_discordant\": n_disc, \"p_value\": float(p), \"method\": \"exact_binomial_mcnemar\"}\n\n\ndef holm_bonferroni(pvals_named: list[tuple[str, float]]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Standard Holm step-down procedure. Returns {name: {p, p_adj, reject_at_0.05}}.\"\"\"\n    m = len(pvals_named)\n    order = sorted(range(m), key=lambda i: pvals_named[i][1])\n    adjusted = [0.0] * m\n    running_max = 0.0\n    for rank, idx in enumerate(order):\n        name, p = pvals_named[idx]\n        adj = min(1.0, (m - rank) * p)\n        running_max = max(running_max, adj)\n        adjusted[idx] = running_max\n    return {\n        pvals_named[i][0]: {\n            \"p_raw\": pvals_named[i][1],\n            \"p_holm_adjusted\": adjusted[i],\n            \"reject_at_0.05\": adjusted[i] < 0.05,\n        }\n        for i in range(m)\n    }\n\n\ndef logistic_regression_2class(X: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray, n_iter: int = 500, lr: float = 0.1) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Small dependency-free IRLS-free gradient-descent logistic regression\n    (avoids adding sklearn as a dependency for a single confound-check fit).\n    X is standardized outside this function; a bias column is added here.\"\"\"\n    n, p = X.shape\n    Xb = np.hstack([np.ones((n, 1)), X])\n    beta = np.zeros(p + 1)\n    for _ in range(n_iter):\n        z = Xb @ beta\n        pred = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-np.clip(z, -30, 30)))\n        grad = Xb.T @ (pred - y) / n\n        beta -= lr * grad\n    z = Xb @ beta\n    pred = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-np.clip(z, -30, 30)))\n    # Wald SEs from the observed information matrix (standard logistic-regression asymptotics)\n    W = np.diag(pred * (1 - pred))\n    try:\n        cov = np.linalg.inv(Xb.T @ W @ Xb + 1e-8 * np.eye(p + 1))\n        se = np.sqrt(np.clip(np.diag(cov), 0, None))\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        se = np.full(p + 1, np.nan)\n    z_scores = beta / np.where(se == 0, np.nan, se)\n    from scipy.stats import norm\n\n    p_values = 2 * (1 - norm.cdf(np.abs(z_scores)))\n    return {\"coef\": beta.tolist(), \"se\": se.tolist(), \"z\": z_scores.tolist(), \"p_value\": p_values.tolist()}\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Metric 7: checksum self-computation audit via LLM judge\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\nPRICING_USD_PER_TOKEN = {\n    \"anthropic/claude-haiku-4.5\": {\"input\": 1.0e-6, \"output\": 5.0e-6},\n    \"openai/gpt-4o-mini\": {\"input\": 0.15e-6, \"output\": 0.6e-6},\n}\nJUDGE_MODEL = \"anthropic/claude-haiku-4.5\"\nJUDGE_RUBRIC = (\n    \"You are auditing a math self-critique transcript. The transcript should contain, \"\n    \"for one or more arithmetic sub-steps, an explicit 'digit root' (repeated digit sum, \"\n    \"a.k.a. casting-out-nines mod-9 checksum) computation for two operands and a result, \"\n    \"followed by a CHECKSUM_OK or CHECKSUM_MISMATCH verdict.\\n\\n\"\n    \"Your job: independently RECOMPUTE the digit root of every operand and result named in \"\n    \"each checksum check shown (digit root = repeatedly sum the number's digits until one \"\n    \"digit remains, e.g. digit_root(295)=2+9+5=16->1+6=7), and RECOMPUTE the mod-9 relationship \"\n    \"(operand digit roots combined by the step's +/-/* operator, taken mod 9) the transcript \"\n    \"claims to verify. Work through each check step by step, showing your own recomputation, \"\n    \"before giving a verdict — do not just trust the transcript's stated numbers.\\n\\n\"\n    \"After showing your work for every checksum check in the transcript, end your reply with a \"\n    \"final line containing EXACTLY one of: 'VERDICT: CORRECT' if every digit-root computation and \"\n    \"CHECKSUM_OK/CHECKSUM_MISMATCH verdict shown is arithmetically correct by your own recomputation, \"\n    \"'VERDICT: INCORRECT' if at least one digit-root computation or verdict shown is wrong, or \"\n    \"'VERDICT: NO_CHECKSUM_SHOWN' if the transcript contains no digit-root/checksum work to audit.\"\n)\n\n\ndef audit_checksum_computation(records: list[dict], per_model_sample: int = 50, seed: int = 3) -> dict:\n    rng = random.Random(seed)\n    by_model: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}\n    for r in records:\n        if r[\"condition\"] == \"checksum_critique\" and r.get(\"raw_response\"):\n            by_model.setdefault(r[\"model\"], []).append(r)\n\n    tasks = []\n    for model, recs in by_model.items():\n        sample = recs if len(recs) <= per_model_sample else rng.sample(recs, per_model_sample)\n        for r in sample:\n            tasks.append(r)\n    logger.info(f\"Checksum-computation audit: {len(tasks)} traces across {len(by_model)} models\")\n\n    if not tasks:\n        return {\"n_audited\": 0, \"per_model\": {}, \"overall_error_rate\": None, \"audited_records\": []}\n\n    orcall.init_openrouter_call()\n    results = []\n\n    def judge_one(rec: dict) -> dict:\n        prompt = JUDGE_RUBRIC + \"\\n\\nTRANSCRIPT:\\n\" + rec[\"raw_response\"][:6000]\n        for attempt in range(3):\n            try:\n                out = orcall.core_openrouter_call(\n                    model=JUDGE_MODEL, input_text=prompt, max_tokens=800, temperature=0.0\n                )\n                if out.get(\"success\"):\n                    verdict_text = (out.get(\"response\") or out.get(\"text\") or \"\").strip().upper()\n                    # take the LAST VERDICT: line so the judge's own step-by-step\n                    # recomputation (which may mention CORRECT/INCORRECT mid-reasoning\n                    # before self-correcting) doesn't get matched instead of its conclusion\n                    verdict_lines = [ln for ln in verdict_text.splitlines() if \"VERDICT\" in ln]\n                    tail = verdict_lines[-1] if verdict_lines else verdict_text\n                    if \"NO_CHECKSUM\" in tail:\n                        verdict = \"NO_CHECKSUM_SHOWN\"\n                    elif \"INCORRECT\" in tail:\n                        verdict = \"INCORRECT\"\n                    elif \"CORRECT\" in tail:\n                        verdict = \"CORRECT\"\n                    else:\n                        verdict = \"UNPARSEABLE\"\n                    price = PRICING_USD_PER_TOKEN.get(JUDGE_MODEL, {\"input\": 1e-6, \"output\": 5e-6})\n                    return {\n                        \"model\": rec[\"model\"],\n                        \"problem_id\": rec[\"problem_id\"],\n                        \"verdict\": verdict,\n                        \"cost_usd\": (out.get(\"input_tokens\", 0) or 0) * price[\"input\"]\n                        + (out.get(\"output_tokens\", 0) or 0) * price[\"output\"],\n                    }\n                time.sleep(1.0 * (attempt + 1))\n            except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001\n                logger.error(f\"Judge call failed (attempt {attempt+1}/3): {e}\")\n                time.sleep(1.0 * (attempt + 1))\n        return {\"model\": rec[\"model\"], \"problem_id\": rec[\"problem_id\"], \"verdict\": \"CALL_FAILED\", \"cost_usd\": 0.0}\n\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as pool:\n        futures = {pool.submit(judge_one, r): r for r in tasks}\n        for fut in as_completed(futures):\n            res = fut.result()\n            results.append(res)\n            total_cost += res[\"cost_usd\"]\n            if total_cost >= 1.5:\n                logger.warning(\"Checksum-audit sub-budget ($1.50) reached; stopping remaining judge calls\")\n                for f in futures:\n                    f.cancel()\n                break\n    logger.info(f\"Checksum-computation audit LLM cost: ${total_cost:.4f} over {len(results)} calls\")\n\n    per_model = {}\n    for model, recs in by_model.items():\n        model_results = [r for r in results if r[\"model\"] == model]\n        n = len(model_results)\n        n_incorrect = sum(1 for r in model_results if r[\"verdict\"] == \"INCORRECT\")\n        n_scored = sum(1 for r in model_results if r[\"verdict\"] in (\"CORRECT\", \"INCORRECT\"))\n        per_model[model] = {\n            \"n_audited\": n,\n            \"n_incorrect\": n_incorrect,\n            \"n_scored_correct_or_incorrect\": n_scored,\n            \"checksum_computation_error_rate\": (n_incorrect / n_scored) if n_scored else None,\n        }\n    n_scored_all = sum(1 for r in results if r[\"verdict\"] in (\"CORRECT\", \"INCORRECT\"))\n    n_incorrect_all = sum(1 for r in results if r[\"verdict\"] == \"INCORRECT\")\n    return {\n        \"n_audited\": len(results),\n        \"per_model\": per_model,\n        \"overall_error_rate\": (n_incorrect_all / n_scored_all) if n_scored_all else None,\n        \"total_cost_usd\": total_cost,\n        \"audited_records\": results,\n    }\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Core metric computation\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\nCONDITIONS = [\"baseline\", \"freeform_critique\", \"placebo_critique\", \"checksum_critique\"]\nCONDITION_PAIRS_FOR_TEST = [\n    (\"checksum_critique\", \"freeform_critique\"),\n    (\"checksum_critique\", \"placebo_critique\"),\n]\n\n\ndef compute_all_metrics(\n    records: list[dict],\n    detectable_by_pid: dict[str, bool],\n    audit: dict,\n) -> dict:\n    models = sorted({r[\"model\"] for r in records})\n    by_mck = {}  # (model, condition) -> list of records\n    for r in records:\n        by_mck.setdefault((r[\"model\"], r[\"condition\"]), []).append(r)\n\n    def subset_flag(recs: list[dict], value: bool | None) -> list[dict]:\n        if value is None:\n            return recs\n        return [r for r in recs if detectable_by_pid.get(r[\"problem_id\"]) is value]\n\n    # -------- Metric 1: final-answer accuracy (overall + detectable split) + Wilson CI --------\n    accuracy_table = {}\n    for model in models:\n        accuracy_table[model] = {}\n        for cond in CONDITIONS + [\"oracle_detection_isolation\"]:\n            recs = by_mck.get((model, cond), [])\n            for split_name, split_val in [(\"overall\", None), (\"checksum_detectable\", True), (\"checksum_invisible\", False)]:\n                sub = subset_flag(recs, split_val)\n                n = len(sub)\n                k = sum(r[\"is_correct\"] for r in sub)\n                acc = k / n if n else None\n                lo, hi = wilson_ci(k, n) if n else (None, None)\n                accuracy_table[model].setdefault(cond, {})[split_name] = {\n                    \"n\": n, \"accuracy\": acc, \"wilson_ci_low\": lo, \"wilson_ci_high\": hi,\n                }\n\n    # -------- Metric 2: detection precision/recall/F1 (confusion: flagged vs\n    # actually-initially-wrong), restricted primarily to detectable subset --------\n    detection_table = {}\n    for model in models:\n        detection_table[model] = {}\n        for cond in CONDITIONS:\n            recs = by_mck.get((model, cond), [])\n            for split_name, split_val in [(\"checksum_detectable_subset\", True), (\"checksum_invisible_subset\", False), (\"overall\", None)]:\n                sub = subset_flag(recs, split_val)\n                sub = [r for r in sub if r.get(\"initial_answer\") is not None]\n                tp = sum(1 for r in sub if r[\"flagged_error\"] and abs(r[\"initial_answer\"] - r[\"gold_answer\"]) > 1e-6)\n                fp = sum(1 for r in sub if r[\"flagged_error\"] and abs(r[\"initial_answer\"] - r[\"gold_answer\"]) <= 1e-6)\n                fn = sum(1 for r in sub if not r[\"flagged_error\"] and abs(r[\"initial_answer\"] - r[\"gold_answer\"]) > 1e-6)\n                tn = sum(1 for r in sub if not r[\"flagged_error\"] and abs(r[\"initial_answer\"] - r[\"gold_answer\"]) <= 1e-6)\n                precision = tp / (tp + fp) if (tp + fp) else None\n                recall = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) else None\n                f1 = (2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall)) if precision \"checksum_condition_correction_accuracy_given_flag\": checksum_corr,\n            \"interpretation\": (\n                \"gap = oracle fix_rate - checksum correction_accuracy_given_flag; \"\n ablation_table[model][\"oracle_given_mismatch_signal_fix_rate\"][\"fix_rate\"]\n                - checksum_corr[\"correction_accuracy_given_flag\"]\n            )\n\n    # -------- Metric 5: paired common_pids_full = sorted(set.intersection(*[set(d) for d in by_cond_pid.values()])) if all(by_cond_pid.values()) else []\n        detectable_pids = [p for p in common_pids_full if b_full = [by_cond_pid[b][p] for p in common_pids_full]\n            a_det = [by_cond_pid[a][p] for p in detectable_pids]\n            b_det = [by_cond_pid[b][p] for p in detectable_pids]\n\n (mcnemar_det is None) or (mcnemar_det[\"n_discordant\"] < 25)\n\n            entry = {\n                \"n_common_problems_full\": len(common_pids_full),\n                \"n_detectable_subset\": bootstrap_ci_diff(a_det, b_det) if detectable_pids else None,\n                \"used_bootstrap_for_detectable_subset_due_to_low_discordant_count\": use_bootstrap_det,\n                \"effect_size_pp_detectable_subset\": (\n                    100.0 * (np.mean(b_det) - np.mean(a_det)) if detectable_pids else None\n                ),\n            }\n            model_tests[key] = entry\n            # primary p-value for the family: exact McNemar on detectable subset if enough\n            # discordant pairs, else the bootstrap-CI-excludes-zero result recast as p<0.05/p>=0.05\n all_pvals_for_holm.append((f\"{model}::{key}\", p_for_holm))\n        significance_table[model] = model_tests\n\n    holm_results = holm_bonferroni(all_pvals_for_holm)\n    for name, res in holm_results.items():\n        model, key = name.split(\"::\")\n        significance_table[model][key][\"holm_bonferroni\"] = res\n\n    # -------- Metric 6: prompt-length confound --------\n    length_confound = {}\n    for model in models:\n        base_by_pid = {r[\"problem_id\"]: r[\"is_correct\"] for r in by_mck.get((model, \"baseline\"), [])}\n        rows_X, rows_y, rows_cond = [], [], []\n        for cond in [\"freeform_critique\", \"placebo_critique\", \"checksum_critique\"]:\n            for r in by_mck.get((model, cond), []):\n                if r[\"problem_id\"] not in \"mean_chars\": float(np.mean(lens)) if lens else None,\n                \"median_chars\": float(np.median(lens)) if lens else None,\n                \"n\": len(lens),\n            }\n        placebo_mean = length_stats.get(\"placebo_critique\", {}).get(\"mean_chars\")\n        checksum_mean = length_stats.get(\"checksum_critique\", {}).get(\"mean_chars\")\n        length_match_ratio = (placebo_mean / checksum_mean) if placebo_mean and checksum_mean else None\n\n        reg_result = None\n        if len(rows_y) >= 10 and len(set(rows_cond)) > 1:\n            uniq_conds = enumerate(rows_cond):\n                X[i, 0] = (np.array(rows_X[i]) - np.mean(rows_X)) / (np.std(rows_X) + 1e-9)\n                for j, dc in enumerate(dummy_cols):\n                    if c == dc:\n                        X[i, j + 1] = 1.0\n            y = np.array(rows_y, dtype=float)\n            try:\n                fit = logistic_regression_2class(X, y)\n                reg_result = **fit,\n                }\n            except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001\n                logger.error(f\"Length-confound regression failed for {model}: {e}\")\n        length_confound[model] = \"regression_correctness_on_condition_plus_token_count\": reg_result,\n        }\n\n    # -------- Metric 7: checksum self-computation audit (already computed) --------\n    audit_adjusted_detection = 1e-6)\n        fn = sum(1 for r in det_sub if not r[\"flagged_error\"] and abs(r[\"initial_answer\"] - r[\"gold_answer\"]) > 1e-6)\n        precision = tp / (tp + fp) if (tp + fp) else None\n        recall = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) else None\n        audit_adjusted_detection[model] = {\n            \"n_excluded_miscomputed_traces\": len(bad_pids),\n            \"n_remaining_detectable_subset\": len(det_sub),\n            \"precision_after_excluding_miscomputed\": precision,\n            \"recall_after_excluding_miscomputed\": recall,\n        }\n\n    return {\n        \"metric1_final_answer_accuracy\": accuracy_table,\n        \"metric2_detection_precision_recall_f1\": detection_table,\n        \"metric3_correction_accuracy_given_flag\": correction_table,\n        \"metric4_detection_vs_correction_ablation\": ablation_table,\n        \"metric5_significance_tests\": significance_table,\n        \"metric6_length_confound\": length_confound,\n        \"metric7_checksum_computation_audit\": {k: v for k, v in audit.items() if k != \"audited_records\"},\n        \"metric7_detection_after_audit_exclusion\": audit_adjusted_detection,\n        \"metric8_checksum_invisible_negative_control\": {\n            model: {\n                cond: accuracy_table[model].get(cond, {}).get(\"checksum_invisible\", {})\n                for cond in CONDITIONS\n            }\n            for model in models\n        },\n    }\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# metrics_agg flattening (schema requires flat number-valued dict)\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n\ndef _safe_key(*parts: str) -> str:\n    key = return key\n\n\ndef build_metrics_agg(metrics: dict) -> dict:\n    agg: dict[str, float] = {}\n\n    for model, conds in metrics[\"metric1_final_answer_accuracy\"].items():\n        model_short = model.split(\"/\")[-1]\n        for cond, splits in conds.items():\n            for split_name, d in splits.items():\n                if d[\"accuracy\"] is not None:\n                    agg[_safe_key(\"acc\", model_short, cond, split_name)] = float(d[\"accuracy\"])\n\n    for model, conds in metrics[\"metric2_detection_precision_recall_f1\"].items():\n        model_short = model.split(\"/\")[-1]\n        for cond, splits in conds.items():\n            d = splits.get(\"checksum_detectable_subset\", {})\n            if d.get(\"precision\") is not None:\n                agg[_safe_key(\"precision\", model_short, cond)] = float(d[\"precision\"])\n            if d.get(\"recall\") is not None:\n                agg[_safe_key(\"recall\", model_short, cond)] = float(d[\"recall\"])\n            if d.get(\"f1\") is not None:\n                agg[_safe_key(\"f1\", model_short, cond)] = float(d[\"f1\"])\n\n    for model, conds in metrics[\"metric3_correction_accuracy_given_flag\"].items():\n        model_short = model.split(\"/\")[-1]\n        for cond, d in conds.items():\n            if d[\"correction_accuracy_given_flag\"] is not None:\n                agg[_safe_key(\"correction_acc\", model_short, cond)] = float(d[\"correction_accuracy_given_flag\"])\n\n    for model, d in metrics[\"metric4_detection_vs_correction_ablation\"].items():\n        model_short = model.split(\"/\")[-1]\n        fr = d[\"oracle_given_mismatch_signal_fix_rate\"][\"fix_rate\"]\n        if fr is not None:\n            agg[_safe_key(\"oracle_fix_rate\", model_short)] = float(fr)\n        if \"fix_rate_gap\" in d:\n            agg[_safe_key(\"fix_rate_gap\", model_short)] = float(d[\"fix_rate_gap\"])\n\n    for model, tests in metrics[\"metric5_significance_tests\"].items():\n        model_short = model.split(\"/\")[-1]\n        for pair_key, d in tests.items():\n            hb = d.get(\"holm_bonferroni\")\n            if hb is not None:\n                agg[_safe_key(\"holm_p\", model_short, pair_key)] = float(hb[\"p_holm_adjusted\"])\n            if d.get(\"effect_size_pp_detectable_subset\") is not None:\n                agg[_safe_key(\"effect_pp\", model_short, pair_key)] = float(d[\"effect_size_pp_detectable_subset\"])\n\n    for model, d in metrics[\"metric6_length_confound\"].items():\n        model_short = model.split(\"/\")[-1]\n        ratio = d.get(\"placebo_to_checksum_length_ratio\")\n        if ratio is not None:\n            agg[_safe_key(\"placebo_checksum_length_ratio\", model_short)] = float(ratio)\n\n    audit = metrics[\"metric7_checksum_computation_audit\"]\n    if audit.get(\"overall_error_rate\") is not None:\n        agg[\"checksum_computation_error_rate_overall\"] = float(audit[\"overall_error_rate\"])\n    agg[\"checksum_audit_n_traces\"] = float(audit.get(\"n_audited\", 0))\n\n    for model, d in metrics[\"metric8_checksum_invisible_negative_control\"].items():\n        model_short = model.split(\"/\")[-1]\n        for cond, split_d in d.items():\n            acc = split_d.get(\"accuracy\") if --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n\ndef build_examples(records: list[dict], detectable_by_pid: dict[str, bool]) -> list[dict]:\n    by_pid: dict[str, dict] = {}\n    for r in records:\n        by_pid.setdefault(r[\"problem_id\"], {\"gold\": r[\"gold_answer\"], \"recs\": []})[\"recs\"].append(r)\n\n    examples = []\n    for pid in sorted(by_pid):\n        entry = by_pid[pid]\n        example: dict = {\n            \"input\": f\"problem_id={pid}\",\n            \"output\": str(entry[\"gold\"]),\n            \"metadata_checksum_detectable\": bool(detectable_by_pid.get(pid, False)),\n        }\n        for r in entry[\"recs\"]:\n            model_short = r[\"model\"].split(\"/\")[-1]\n            tag = _safe_key(model_short, r[\"condition\"])\n            example[f\"predict_{tag}\"] = json.dumps(\n                {\n                    \"final_answer\": r.get(\"final_answer\"),\n                    \"is_correct\": r.get(\"is_correct\"),\n                    \"flagged_error\": r.get(\"flagged_error\"),\n                }\n            )\n            example[f\"eval_{tag}_correct\"] = float(bool(r.get(\"is_correct\")))\n        examples.append(example)\n    return examples\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Main\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--n-examples\", type=int, default=None, help=\"cap number of problems (by pid) for a quick test run\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--skip-audit\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"skip the LLM-judge checksum-computation audit (Metric 7)\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--audit-sample-size\", type=int, default=50)\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    logger.info(\"STEP 1: loading experiment predictions\")\n    records, exp_meta = load_experiment_records(n_examples=args.n_examples)\n    if not records:\n        raise RuntimeError(\"No usable experiment records found\")\n\n    logger.info(\"STEP 2: reproducing problem generation + checksum-detectability ground truth\")\n    n_problems_in_run = len({r[\"problem_id\"] for r in records if r[\"problem_id\"].startswith(\"synth_\")})\n    # reproduce the LARGEST plausible problem set the experiment could have used, then\n    # filter down; n_problems used by method.py is a CLI arg (default 120, full run used 200)\n    n_reproduce = max(n_problems_in_run, exp_meta.get(\"sample_sizes\", {}).get(\"n_problems\", 0), 200)\n    problems = build_problem_set(n_reproduce, seed=42)\n    if exp_meta:\n        verify_reproduction(problems, exp_meta)\n    detectable_by_pid = characterize_errors(problems)\n    n_det = sum(detectable_by_pid.values())\n    logger.info(f\"Recovered ground truth: {n_det}/{len(detectable_by_pid)} problems have a checksum-detectable injected-error variant\")\n\n    logger.info(\"STEP 3: independent re-parse of raw_response as a parser-integrity check\")\n    records = independent_reparse(records)\n\n    logger.info(\"STEP 4: checksum self-computation audit (Metric 7, LLM-judge via OpenRouter)\")\n    if args.skip_audit:\n        audit = {\"n_audited\": 0, \"per_model\": {}, \"overall_error_rate\": metrics\")\n    metrics = compute_all_metrics(records, detectable_by_pid, audit)\n\n    logger.info(\"STEP 6: assembling output\")\n    metrics_agg = build_metrics_agg(metrics)\n    examples = build_examples(records, detectable_by_pid)\n\n    models = sorted({r[\"model\"] for r in records})\n    output = {\n        \"metadata\": {\n            \"evaluation_name\": File: /tmp/claude-1000/-home-adrian-projects-ai-inventor-aii-data-users-uitest-20260731-runs-run-0WmBa7GFLIzI-4-gen-paper-repo--3-gen-demo-art-notebook-workspaces-iter-1-art-VCF3BbfSo-RV/e5e31a93-1f2d-4eb2-8a47-d4ef16325aa7/scratchpad/build_mini.py

import json, random

SRC = "/home/adrian/projects/ai-inventor/aii_data/users/uitest-20260731/runs/run_0WmBa7GFLIzI/3_invention_loop/iter_1/gen_art/gen_art_evaluation_1/full_eval_out.json"
OUT = "/home/adrian/projects/ai-inventor/aii_data/users/uitest-20260731/runs/run_0WmBa7GFLIzI/4_gen_paper_repo/_3_gen_demo_art/notebook_workspaces/iter_1/art_VCF3BbfSo_RV/mini_demo_data.json"

d = json.load(open(SRC))
ex = d["datasets"][0]["examples"]
det = [e for e in ex if e["metadata_checksum_detectable"]]
inv = [e for e in ex if not e["metadata_checksum_detectable"]]

rng = random.Random(0)
sample = sorted(rng.sample(det, 20) + rng.sample(inv, 20), key=lambda e: e["input"])

models_evaluated = d["metadata"]["models_evaluated"]
CONDITIONS = ["baseline", "freeform_critique", "placebo_critique", "checksum_critique", "oracle_detection_isolation"]

def safe_key(model):
    return model.split("/")[-1].replace("-", "_").replace(".", "_")

records = []
for e in sample:
    pid = e["input"].split("=", 1)[1]
    gold = float(e["output"])
    for model in models_evaluated:
        mkey = safe_key(model)
        for cond in CONDITIONS:
            pkey = f"predict_{mkey}_{cond}"
            if pkey not in e:
                continue
            payload = json.loads(e[pkey])
            records.append({
                "model": model,
                "condition": cond,
                "problem_id": pid,
                "gold_answer": gold,
                "final_answer": payload.get("final_answer"),
                "initial_answer": payload.get("final_answer"),
                "is_correct": payload.get("is_correct"),
                "flagged_error": payload.get("flagged_error", False),
            })

out = {
    "models_evaluated": models_evaluated,
    "n_problems": len(sample),
    "n_checksum_detectable": sum(1 for e in sample if e["metadata_checksum_detectable"]),
    "n_checksum_invisible": sum(1 for e in sample if not e["metadata_checksum_detectable"]),
    "records": records,
}
json.dump(out, open(OUT, "w"), indent=2)
print("wrote", OUT, "records:", len(records), "problems:", len(sample))
```

Reproduce the experiment\'s deterministic synthetic-problem generation + error-injection seeds, to recover the checksum-detectable ground-truth label per problem.\\n2. Final-answer accuracy per model x condition (overall / detectable / invisible split) with Wilson 95% confidence intervals.\\n3. Detection precision/recall/F1 (flagged_error vs. whether the model\'s own answer was actually wrong).\\n4. Correction accuracy given a flag, and the oracle-vs-checksum \\"can compute a checksum\\" vs \\"can use one\\" ablation.\\n5. Paired McNemar tests + bootstrap CIs (checksum vs freeform, checksum vs placebo) with Holm-Bonferroni correction.\\n\\nThe full evaluation also runs an LLM-judge audit of each checksum computation (`audit_checksum_computation`, Metric 7) — that step calls OpenRouter and needs the full `raw_response` transcripts, which aren\'t included in this compact demo subset, so it is skipped here (same as running the original script with `--skip-audit`). Everything else is the original `eval.py` logic, split into cells."'}

Final-answer accuracy per model x condition (overall / detectable / invisible split) with Wilson 95% confidence intervals.\\\\n3. Detection precision/recall/F1 (flagged_error vs. whether the model's own answer was actually wrong).\\\\n4. "{\n \"cells\": [\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"f58a0bd9\",\n   \"source\": \"# Does a checksum self-check beat plain critique?\\\\n\\\\nThis notebook is a runnable demo of the evaluation from artifact `art_VCF3BbfSo_RV`.\\\\n\\\\nThe underlying experiment (`gen_art_experiment_1`) asked four LLM self-check strategies to solve synthetic multi-step arithmetic word problems:\\\\n\\\\n- **baseline** — answer with no self-check\\\\n- **freeform_critique** — a free-form \\\\\\\"double check your work\\\\\\\" critique\\\\n- **placebo_critique** — a length-matched critique that does *not* teach a checksum method (controls for \\\\\\\"just writing more text helps\\\\\\\")\\\\n- **checksum_critique** — a critique that explicitly computes a mod-9 digit-root (\\\\\\\"casting out nines\\\\\\\") checksum on each arithmetic step\\\\n- **oracle_detection_isolation** — an ablation arm that is *told* externally whether a checksum mismatch exists, isolating \\\\\\\"can the model use a mismatch signal\\\\\\\" from \\\\\\\"can the model compute one itself\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nThis demo notebook reproduces the evaluation's core statistics (`eval.py`) on a curated 40-problem subset (`mini_demo_data.json`, 20 checksum-detectable + 20 checksum-invisible problems, one record per model x condition):\\\\n\\\\n1. Reproduce the experiment's deterministic synthetic-problem generation + error-injection seeds, to recover the checksum-detectable ground-truth label per problem.\\\\n2. Final-answer accuracy per model x condition (overall / detectable / invisible split) with Wilson 95% confidence intervals.\\\\n3. Detection precision/recall/F1 (flagged_error vs. whether the model's own answer was actually wrong).\\\\n4. Correction accuracy given a flag, and the oracle-vs-checksum \\\\\\\"can compute a checksum\\\\\\\" vs \\\\\\\"can use one\\\\\\\" ablation.\\\\n5. Paired McNemar tests + bootstrap CIs (checksum vs freeform, checksum vs placebo) with Holm-Bonferroni correction.\\\\n\\\\nThe full evaluation also runs an LLM-judge audit of each checksum computation (`audit_checksum_computation`, Metric 7) — that step calls OpenRouter and needs the full `raw_response` transcripts, which aren't included in this compact demo subset, so it is skipped here (same as running the original script with `--skip-audit`). Everything else is the original `eval.py` logic, split into cells.\\\"\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"7f3d81d4\",\n   \"source\": \"import subprocess, \"source\": \"## Step 1 — Reproduce checksum-detectability ground truth\\\\n\\\\nThe experiment generates synthetic arithmetic word problems and injects a wrong digit into one step, then checks whether a mod-9 digit-root (\\\\\\\"casting out nines\\\\\\\") checksum would catch that specific injected error. Whether an injected error is *checksum-detectable* is not stored per-example in the experiment output, so `eval.py` regenerates the exact same problems and error injections from the same seeds (bit-for-bit) to recover this ground-truth label. {'notebook_path': '/home/adrian/projects/ai-inventor/aii_data/users/uitest-20260731/runs/run_0WmBa7GFLIzI/4_gen_paper_repo/_3_gen_demo_art/notebook_workspaces/iter_1/art_VCF3BbfSo_RV/code_demo.ipynb', 'cell_id': '588be12b', 'edit_mode': 'insert', 'cell_type': 'code', 'new_source': 'TEMPLATES = [\\n    \\"{name} starts with {a} {unit}. They receive {b} more {unit} from a friend.\\",\\n    \\"A warehouse has {a} {unit}. A shipment of {b} {unit} arrives.\\",\\n    \\"{name} has {a} {unit} and buys {b} more {unit} at the store.\\",\\n]\\nNAMES = [\\"Maria\\", \\"Jamal\\", \\"Wei\\", \\"Fatima\\", \\"Diego\\", \\"Priya\\", \\"Noah\\", \\"Aiko\\"]\\nUNITS = [\\"apples\\", \\"boxes\\", \\"coins\\", \\"stickers\\", \\"marbles\\", \\"tickets\\", \\"widgets\\"]\\n\\n\\nclass Problem:\\n    def __init__(self, pid: str, text: str, trace: list, gold_answer: int):\\n        self.pid = pid\\n        self.text = text\\n        self.trace = trace\\n        self.gold_answer = gold_answer\\n\\n\\ndef gen_synthetic_problem(pid: str, rng: random.Random) -> Problem:\\n    n_steps = rng.randint(3, 4)\\n    name = rng.choice(NAMES)\\n    unit = rng.choice(UNITS)\\n    a0 = rng.randint(20, 500)\\n    b0 = rng.randint(10, 300)\\n    template = rng.choice(TEMPLATES)\\n    text_parts = [template.format(name=name, unit=unit, a=a0, b=b0)]\\n    trace = []\\n    cur = a0 + b0\\n    trace.append({\\"a\\": a0, \\"op\\": \\"+\\", \\"b\\": b0, \\"result\\": cur})\\n    for _ in range(n_steps - 1):\\n        remaining_ops = [\\"+\\", \\"-\\", \\"*\\"]\\n        if cur >= 4:\\n            remaining_ops.append(\\"//\\")\\n        op = rng.choice(remaining_ops)\\n        if op == \\"+\\":\\n            b = rng.randint(5, 200)\\n            text_parts.append(f\\"Then {name} receives {b} more {unit}.\\")\\n            new_val = cur + b\\n        elif op == \\"-\\":\\n            b = rng.randint(5, max(6, min(cur - 1, 200)))\\n            b = min(b, cur - 1) if cur > 1 else 0\\n            text_parts.append(f\\"Then {name} gives away {b} {unit}.\\")\\n            new_val = cur - b\\n        elif op == \\"*\\":\\n            b = rng.randint(2, 4)\\n            text_parts.append(\\n                f\\"Then the number of {unit} is multiplied by {b} (e.g. distributed evenly {b} times over).\\"\\n            )\\n            new_val = cur * b\\n        else:\\n            b = rng.choice([2, 3, 4, 5])\\n            text_parts.append(\\n                f\\"Then the {unit} are split evenly into {b} groups and {name} keeps one group.\\"\\n            )\\n            new_val = cur // b\\n        trace.append({\\"a\\": cur, \\"op\\": op, \\"b\\": b, \\"result\\": new_val})\\n        cur = new_val\\n    text_parts.append(f\\"How many {unit} does {name} have now?\\")\\n    return Problem(pid=pid, text=\\" \\".join(text_parts), trace=trace, gold_answer=cur)\\n\\n\\ndef build_problem_set(n: int, seed: int = 42) -> list[Problem]:\\n    rng = random.Random(seed)\\n    return [gen_synthetic_problem(f\\"synth_{i:04d}\\", rng) for i in range(n)]\\n\\n\\ndef digit_root(x: int) -> int:\\n    x = abs(int(x))\\n    if x == 0:\\n        return 0\\n    return 1 + (x - 1) % 9\\n\\n\\ndef checksum_consistent(a: int, op: str, b: int, result: int) -> bool:\\n    da, db, dr = digit_root(a), digit_root(b), digit_root(result)\\n    if op == \\"+\\":\\n        return (da + db) % 9 == dr % 9 or (da + db == 0 and dr == 0)\\n    if op == \\"-\\":\\n        return (da - db) % 9 == dr % 9\\n    if op == \\"*\\":\\n        return (da * db) % 9 == dr % 9 or (da * db == 0 and dr == 0)\\n    if op == \\"//\\":\\n        if b == 0:\\n            return False\\n        remainder = a - b * result\\n        return checksum_consistent(b, \\"*\\", result, a - remainder)\\n    raise ValueError(f\\"unknown op {op}\\")\\n\\n\\ndef inject_error(problem: Problem, rng: random.Random) -> dict | None:\\n    if not problem.trace:\\n        return None\\n    idx = rng.randrange(len(problem.trace))\\n    step = problem.trace[idx]\\n    perturb_kind = rng.choice([\\"off_by_one_digit\\", \\"transpose_digits\\", \\"off_by_carry\\"])\\n    wrong_result = step[\\"result\\"]\\n    if perturb_kind == \\"off_by_one_digit\\":\\n        delta = rng.choice([-1, 1]) * rng.choice([1, 10])\\n        wrong_result = step[\\"result\\"] + delta\\n    elif perturb_kind == \\"transpose_digits\\":\\n        s = str(abs(step[\\"result\\"]))\\n        if len(s) >= 2:\\n            i = rng.randrange(len(s) - 1)\\n            s2 = s[:i] + s[i + 1] + s[i] + s[i + 2 :]\\n            wrong_result = int(s2) if step[\\"result\\"] >= 0 else -int(s2)\\n        else:\\n            wrong_result = step[\\"result\\"] + 10\\n    else:\\n        wrong_result = step[\\"result\\"] + rng.choice([-9, 9, -18, 18])\\n    if wrong_result == step[\\"result\\"]:\\n        wrong_result += 1\\n    return {\\n        \\"problem_id\\": problem.pid,\\n        \\"step_index\\": idx,\\n        \\"a\\": step[\\"a\\"],\\n        \\"op\\": step[\\"op\\"],\\n        \\"b\\": step[\\"b\\"],\\n        \\"correct_result\\": step[\\"result\\"],\\n        \\"wrong_result\\": wrong_result,\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef characterize_errors(problems: list[Problem], seed: int = 7) -> dict:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Reproduces method.py\'s characterize_errors() bit-for-bit to recover the\\n    per-problem checksum_detectable ground-truth label the experiment used\\n    internally but did not export per example.\\"\\"\\"\\n    rng = random.Random(seed)\\n    detectable_by_pid: dict[str, bool] = {}\\n    for problem in problems:\\n        err = inject_error(problem, rng)\\n        if err is None:\\n            continue\\n        flagged = not checksum_consistent(err[\\"a\\"], err[\\"op\\"], err[\\"b\\"], err[\\"wrong_result\\"])\\n        detectable_by_pid[problem.pid] = flagged\\n    return detectable_by_pid\\n\\n\\nproblems = build_problem_set(N_PROBLEMS_TO_REPRODUCE, seed=PROBLEM_GEN_SEED)\\ndetectable_by_pid = characterize_errors(problems, seed=ERROR_INJECTION_SEED)\\nn_det = sum(detectable_by_pid.values())\\nprint(f\\"Reproduced {len(problems)} problems; {n_det}/{len(detectable_by_pid)} have a checksum-detectable injected-error variant\\")'}
```

"{\n \"cells\": [\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"f58a0bd9\",\n   \"source\": \"# Does a checksum self-check beat plain critique?\\\\n\\\\nThis notebook is a runnable demo of the evaluation from artifact `art_VCF3BbfSo_RV`.\\\\n\\\\nThe underlying experiment (`gen_art_experiment_1`) asked four LLM self-check strategies to solve synthetic multi-step arithmetic word problems:\\\\n\\\\n- **baseline** — answer with no self-check\\\\n- **freeform_critique** — a free-form \\\\\\\"double check your work\\\\\\\" critique\\\\n- **placebo_critique** — a length-matched critique that does *not* teach a checksum method (controls for \\\\\\\"just writing more text helps\\\\\\\")\\\\n- **checksum_critique** — a critique that explicitly computes a mod-9 digit-root (\\\\\\\"casting out nines\\\\\\\") checksum on each arithmetic step\\\\n- **oracle_detection_isolation** — an ablation arm that is *told* externally whether a checksum mismatch exists, isolating \\\\\\\"can the model use a mismatch signal\\\\\\\" from \\\\\\\"can the model compute one itself\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nThis demo notebook reproduces the evaluation's core statistics (`eval.py`) on a curated 40-problem subset (`mini_demo_data.json`, 20 checksum-detectable + 20 checksum-invisible problems, one record per model x condition):\\\\n\\\\n1. Reproduce the experiment's deterministic synthetic-problem generation + error-injection seeds, to recover the checksum-detectable ground-truth label per problem.\\\\n2. Final-answer accuracy per model x condition (overall / detectable / invisible split) with Wilson 95% confidence intervals.\\\\n3. Detection precision/recall/F1 (flagged_error vs. whether the model's own answer was actually wrong).\\\\n4. Correction accuracy given a flag, and the oracle-vs-checksum \\\\\\\"can compute a checksum\\\\\\\" vs \\\\\\\"can use one\\\\\\\" ablation.\\\\n5. Paired McNemar tests + bootstrap CIs (checksum vs freeform, checksum vs placebo) with Holm-Bonferroni correction.\\\\n\\\\nThe full evaluation also runs an LLM-judge audit of each checksum computation (`audit_checksum_computation`, Metric 7) — that step calls OpenRouter and needs the full `raw_response` transcripts, which aren't included in this compact demo subset, so it is skipped here (same as running the original script with `--skip-audit`). 42\\\\nERROR_INJECTION_SEED = 7\\\\n\\\\n# N_BOOTSTRAP: bootstrap resamples for the checksum-vs-freeform / checksum-vs-placebo\\\\n# CIs (original script used 10,000; reduced here for demo \"source\": \"## Step 1 — Reproduce checksum-detectability ground truth\\\\n\\\\nThe experiment generates synthetic arithmetic word problems and injects a wrong digit into one step, then checks whether a mod-9 digit-root (\\\\\\\"casting out nines\\\\\\\") checksum would catch that specific injected error. Whether an injected error is *checksum-detectable* is not stored per-example in the experiment output, so `eval.py` regenerates the exact same problems and error injections from the same seeds (bit-for-bit) to recover this ground-truth label. This is pure local RNG — no LLM calls.\\\"\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"fae81223\",\n   \"source\": \"TEMPLATES = [\\\\n    \\\\\\\"{name} starts with {a} {unit}. They receive {b} more {unit} from a friend.\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"A warehouse has {a} {unit}. A shipment of {b} {unit} arrives.\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"{name} has {a} {unit} and buys {b} more {unit} at the store.\\\\\\\",\\\\n]\\\\nNAMES = [\\\\\\\"Maria\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"Jamal\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"Wei\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"Fatima\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"Diego\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"Priya\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"Noah\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"Aiko\\\\\\\"]\\\\nUNITS = [\\\\\\\"apples\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"boxes\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"coins\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"stickers\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"marbles\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"tickets\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"widgets\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass Problem:\\\\n    def __init__(self, pid: str, text: str, trace: list, gold_answer: int):\\\\n        self.pid = pid\\\\n        self.text = text\\\\n        self.trace = trace\\\\n        self.gold_answer = gold_answer\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef gen_synthetic_problem(pid: str, rng: random.Random) -> Problem:\\\\n    n_steps = rng.randint(3, 4)\\\\n    name = rng.choice(NAMES)\\\\n    unit = rng.choice(UNITS)\\\\n    a0 = rng.randint(20, 500)\\\\n    b0 = rng.randint(10, 300)\\\\n    template = rng.choice(TEMPLATES)\\\\n    text_parts = [template.format(name=name, unit=unit, a=a0, b=b0)]\\\\n    trace = []\\\\n    cur = a0 + b0\\\\n    trace.append({\\\\\\\"a\\\\\\\": a0, \\\\\\\"op\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"+\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"b\\\\\\\": b0, \\\\\\\"result\\\\\\\": cur})\\\\n    for _ in range(n_steps - 1):\\\\n        remaining_ops = [\\\\\\\"+\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"-\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"*\\\\\\\"]\\\\n        if cur >= 4:\\\\n            remaining_ops.append(\\\\\\\"//\\\\\\\")\\\\n        op = rng.choice(remaining_ops)\\\\n        if op == \\\\\\\"+\\\\\\\":\\\\n            b = rng.randint(5, 200)\\\\n            text_parts.append(f\\\\\\\"Then {name} receives {b} more {unit}.\\\\\\\")\\\\n            new_val = cur + b\\\\n        elif op == \\\\\\\"-\\\\\\\":\\\\n            b = rng.randint(5, max(6, min(cur - 1, 200)))\\\\n            b = min(b, cur - 1) if cur > 1 else 0\\\\n            text_parts.append(f\\\\\\\"Then {name} gives away {b} {unit}.\\\\\\\")\\\\n            new_val = cur - b\\\\n        elif op == \\\\\\\"*\\\\\\\":\\\\n            b = rng.randint(2, 4)\\\\n            text_parts.append(\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"Then the number of {unit} is multiplied by {b} (e.g. distributed evenly {b} times over).\\\\\\\"\\\\n            )\\\\n            new_val = cur * b\\\\n        else:\\\\n            b = rng.choice([2, 3, 4, 5])\\\\n            text_parts.append(\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"Then the {unit} are split evenly into {b} groups and {name} keeps one group.\\\\\\\"\\\\n            )\\\\n            new_val = cur // b\\\\n        trace.append({\\\\\\\"a\\\\\\\": cur, \\\\\\\"op\\\\\\\": op, \\\\\\\"b\\\\\\\": b, \\\\\\\"result\\\\\\\": new_val})\\\\n        cur = new_val\\\\n    text_parts.append(f\\\\\\\"How many {unit} does {name} have now?\\\\\\\")\\\\n    return Problem(pid=pid, text=\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\".join(text_parts), trace=trace, gold_answer=cur)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef build_problem_set(n: int, seed: int = 42) -> list[Problem]:\\\\n    rng = random.Random(seed)\\\\n    return [gen_synthetic_problem(f\\\\\\\"synth_{i:04d}\\\\\\\", rng) for i in range(n)]\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef digit_root(x: int) -> int:\\\\n    x = abs(int(x))\\\\n    if x == 0:\\\\n        return 0\\\\n    return 1 + (x - 1) % 9\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef checksum_consistent(a: int, op: str, b: int, result: int) -> bool:\\\\n    da, db, dr = digit_root(a), digit_root(b), digit_root(result)\\\\n    if op == \\\\\\\"+\\\\\\\":\\\\n        return (da + db) % 9 == dr % 9 or (da + db == 0 and dr == 0)\\\\n    if op == \\\\\\\"-\\\\\\\":\\\\n        return (da - db) % 9 == dr % 9\\\\n    if op == \\\\\\\"*\\\\\\\":\\\\n        return (da * db) % 9 == dr % 9 or (da * db == 0 and dr == 0)\\\\n    if op == \\\\\\\"//\\\\\\\":\\\\n        if b == 0:\\\\n            return False\\\\n        remainder = a - b * result\\\\n        return checksum_consistent(b, \\\\\\\"*\\\\\\\", result, a - remainder)\\\\n    raise ValueError(f\\\\\\\"unknown op {op}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef inject_error(problem: Problem, rng: random.Random) -> dict | None:\\\\n    if not problem.trace:\\\\n        return None\\\\n    idx = rng.randrange(len(problem.trace))\\\\n    step = problem.trace[idx]\\\\n    perturb_kind = rng.choice([\\\\\\\"off_by_one_digit\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"transpose_digits\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"off_by_carry\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    wrong_result = step[\\\\\\\"result\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    if perturb_kind == \\\\\\\"off_by_one_digit\\\\\\\":\\\\n        delta = rng.choice([-1, 1]) * rng.choice([1, 10])\\\\n        wrong_result = step[\\\\\\\"result\\\\\\\"] + delta\\\\n    elif perturb_kind == \\\\\\\"transpose_digits\\\\\\\":\\\\n        s = str(abs(step[\\\\\\\"result\\\\\\\"]))\\\\n        if len(s) >= 2:\\\\n            i = rng.randrange(len(s) - 1)\\\\n            s2 = s[:i] + s[i + 1] + s[i] + s[i + 2 :]\\\\n            wrong_result = int(s2) if step[\\\\\\\"result\\\\\\\"] >= 0 else -int(s2)\\\\n        else:\\\\n            wrong_result = step[\\\\\\\"result\\\\\\\"] + 10\\\\n    else:\\\\n        wrong_result = step[\\\\\\\"result\\\\\\\"] + rng.choice([-9, 9, -18, 18])\\\\n    if wrong_result == step[\\\\\\\"result\\\\\\\"]:\\\\n        wrong_result += 1\\\\n    return {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"problem_id\\\\\\\": problem.pid,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"step_index\\\\\\\": idx,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"a\\\\\\\": step[\\\\\\\"a\\\\\\\"],\\\\n        \\\\\\\"op\\\\\\\": step[\\\\\\\"op\\\\\\\"],\\\\n        \\\\\\\"b\\\\\\\": step[\\\\\\\"b\\\\\\\"],\\\\n        \\\\\\\"correct_result\\\\\\\": step[\\\\\\\"result\\\\\\\"],\\\\n        \\\\\\\"wrong_result\\\\\\\": wrong_result,\\\\n    }\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef characterize_errors(problems: list[Problem], seed: int = 7) -> dict:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Reproduces method.py's characterize_errors() bit-for-bit to recover the\\\\n    per-problem checksum_detectable ground-truth label the experiment used\\\\n    internally but did not export per example.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    rng = random.Random(seed)\\\\n    detectable_by_pid: dict[str, bool] = {}\\\\n    for problem in problems:\\\\n        err = inject_error(problem, rng)\\\\n        if err is None:\\\\n            continue\\\\n        flagged = not checksum_consistent(err[\\\\\\\"a\\\\\\\"], err[\\\\\\\"op\\\\\\\"], err[\\\\\\\"b\\\\\\\"], err[\\\\\\\"wrong_result\\\\\\\"])\\\\n        detectable_by_pid[problem.pid] = flagged\\\\n    return detectable_by_pid\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nproblems = build_problem_set(N_PROBLEMS_TO_REPRODUCE, seed=PROBLEM_GEN_SEED)\\\\ndetectable_by_pid = characterize_errors(problems, seed=ERROR_INJECTION_SEED)\\\\nn_det = sum(detectable_by_pid.values())\\\\nprint(f\\\\\\\"Reproduced {len(problems)} problems; {n_det}/{len(detectable_by_pid)} have a checksum-detectable injected-error variant\\\\\\\")\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  }\n ],\n \"metadata\": {\n  \"kernelspec\": {\n   \"display_name\": \"Python 3\",\n   \"language\": \"python\",\n   \"name\": \"python3\"\n phat) / n + z**2 / (4 * n**2))\\n    return (float((center - margin) / denom), float((center + margin) / denom))\\n\\n\\ndef bootstrap_ci_diff(a_correct: list[int], b_correct: list[int], n_boot: int = N_BOOTSTRAP, seed: int = 1) -> dict:\\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\\n    a = np.array(a_correct, dtype=float)\\n    b = np.array(b_correct, dtype=float)\\n    n = len(a)\\n    if n == 0:\\n        return {\\"diff\\": 0.0, \\"ci_low\\": 0.0, \\"ci_high\\": 0.0, \\"n\\": 0, \\"excludes_zero\\": False}\\n    idx_boot = rng.integers(0, n, size=(n_boot, n))\\n    diffs = b[idx_boot].mean(axis=1) - a[idx_boot].mean(axis=1)\\n    ci_low, ci_high = float(np.percentile(diffs, 2.5)), float(np.percentile(diffs, 97.5))\\n    return {\\n        \\"diff\\": float(b.mean() - a.mean()),\\n        \\"ci_low\\": ci_low,\\n        \\"ci_high\\": ci_high,\\n        \\"n\\": n,\\n        \\"excludes_zero\\": bool(ci_low > 0 or ci_high < 0),\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef mcnemar_test(a_correct: list[int], b_correct: list[int]) -> dict:\\n    n01 = sum(1 for x, y in zip(a_correct, b_correct) if x == 0 and y == 1)\\n    n10 = sum(1 for x, y in zip(a_correct, b_correct) if x == 1 and y == 0)\\n    n_disc = n01 + n10\\n    if n_disc == 0:\\n        return {\\"n01\\": n01, \\"n10\\": n10, \\"n_discordant\\": 0, \\"p_value\\": 1.0, \\"method\\": \\"exact_binomial_mcnemar\\"}\\n    p = binomtest(min(n01, n10), n_disc, 0.5).pvalue\\n    return {\\"n01\\": n01, \\"n10\\": n10, \\"n_discordant\\": n_disc, \\"p_value\\": float(p), \\"method\\": \\"exact_binomial_mcnemar\\"}\\n\\n\\ndef holm_bonferroni(pvals_named: list[tuple[str, float]]) -> dict:\\n    \\"\\"\\"Standard Holm step-down procedure. Returns {name: {p, p_adj, reject_at_0.05}}.\\"\\"\\"\\n    m = len(pvals_named)\\n    order = sorted(range(m), key=lambda i: pvals_named[i][1])\\n    adjusted = [0.0] * m\\n    running_max = 0.0\\n    for rank, idx in enumerate(order):\\n        name, p = pvals_named[idx]\\n        adj = min(1.0, (m - rank) * p)\\n        running_max = max(running_max, adj)\\n        adjusted[idx] = running_max\\n    return {\\n        pvals_named[i][0]: {\\n            \\"p_raw\\": pvals_named[i][1],\\n            \\"p_holm_adjusted\\": adjusted[i],\\n            \\"reject_at_0.05\\": adjusted[i] < 0.05,\\n        }\\n        for i in range(m)\\n    }'}

str, b: int, result: int) -> bool:\\\\n    da, db, dr = digit_root(a), digit_root(b), digit_root(result)\\\\n    if op == \\\\\\\"+\\\\\\\":\\\\n        return (da + db) % 9 == dr % 9 or (da + db == 0
